4. TRIGGER & GATES
The S4 panel reports no PA pattern firing on MOCAPITAL yet. We have a green candle closing in the upper half of its range, and an RV of 1.20 (stronger than the median), confirming participation is improving. Volume is improving (RV 1.20). Order flow shows 20.9K buyers vs 15.9K sellers on the current look. Cumulative 20-bar delta is negative, but the price is holding at the DZ—this is a classic sign of absorption (trapped shorts providing fuel). No F&O data exists for MOCAPITAL; it is cash-only.

S5 confirms the location is valid (DZ) and that price is in the lower Value Area. The lack of a sweep or Wyckoff base in the last three bars reinforces that this is a direct pullback-to-zone trade rather than a complex range-edge manipulation.

7. THE PANEL'S OWN PLAN
The panel recommends "WAIT for GO" and to stay in a positional capacity (due to Stage 2 context). It sets the invalidation at 51.76 (the DZ distal), which is 3.1% from the current price. The plan is coherent with the location-first doctrine.

8. CONTRADICTIONS
The S4 dashboard marks a "NO TRADE" based on the daily downtrend, but the S4 Trigger row correctly identifies the DZ location and the Stage 2 weekly structure. I side with the Trigger row: we are buying a pull-back to a structural zone, acknowledging that the daily is currently extended/weak.
